Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any unsupervised module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 483. The projective resolution has length at most 807 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to unsupervised structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 483/807 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.
